<script>
        // Subtle micro-interaction for the card on hover
        const card = document.querySelector('.cr80-card');
        
        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 25;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 25;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg) scale(1.02)`;
            card.style.transition = 'transform 0.1s ease-out';
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) scale(1)`;
            card.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease-out';
        });
    </script>

<!-- The user is viewing a specific entity card, making it a "Details/Success" view -->
<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the card following mouse tilt
        const card = document.querySelector('.group');
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (!card) return;
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 20;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 20;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            if (!card) return;
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<!-- (Normally hidden on Detail pages per rules) -->
<script>
        // Micro-interaction for ID Card hover
        const card = document.querySelector('.id-card-bleed');
        if (card) {
            card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                
                const centerX = rect.width / 2;
                const centerY = rect.height / 2;
                
                const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 20;
                const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 20;
                
                card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
            });
            
            card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
                card.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease';
            });

            card.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
                card.style.transition = 'none';
            });
        }
    </script>

<script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const card = document.querySelector('.card-container');
            
            // Subtle tilt effect on mouse move
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;
                
                const xAxis = (window.innerWidth / 2 - e.pageX) / 45;
                const yAxis = (window.innerHeight / 2 - e.pageY) / 45;
                
                card.style.transform = `rotateY(${xAxis}deg) rotateX(${yAxis}deg)`;
            });

            // Smooth transition for card hover
            card.style.transition = 'transform 0.1s ease-out, box-shadow 0.3s ease';
            
            card.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
                card.style.boxShadow = '0 35px 60px -15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)';
            });

            card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                card.style.transform = `rotateY(0deg) rotateX(0deg)`;
                card.style.boxShadow = '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)';
            });
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for card tilt effect (Desktop only)
        const card = document.querySelector('.cr80-card');
        const container = card.parentElement;

        container.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;
            const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 20;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 20;

            card.style.transform = `rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        container.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = `rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<!-- Micro-interactions Script -->
<script>
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const card = document.querySelector('.prism-mesh');
            if (!card) return;
            
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 25;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 25;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        document.querySelector('.prism-mesh')?.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            const card = document.querySelector('.prism-mesh');
            if (card) card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<!-- Micro-interaction Script -->
<script>
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.glass-card');
            const mouseX = e.clientX;
            const mouseY = e.clientY;

            cards.forEach(card => {
                const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
                const cardX = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
                const cardY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;

                const angleX = (mouseY - cardY) / 60;
                const angleY = (cardX - mouseX) / 60;

                // Subtle tilt effect for the ID card only for extra premium feel
                if (card.parentElement.classList.contains('justify-center')) {
                    card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${angleX}deg) rotateY(${angleY}deg)`;
                }
            });
        });

        // Add a pulsing effect to the hologram
        const hologram = document.querySelector('.hologram');
        if(hologram) {
            let opacity = 0.4;
            let direction = 1;
            setInterval(() => {
                opacity += 0.01 * direction;
                if (opacity >= 0.6 || opacity <= 0.2) direction *= -1;
                hologram.style.opacity = opacity;
            }, 50);
        }
    </script>

<script>
        // Subtle interaction: tilt effect for the ID card
        const card = document.querySelector('.group');
        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 10;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 10;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg) scale(1.02)`;
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg) scale(1)';
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        // Simple logic for card interactions or atmospheric effects
        document.querySelector('.cr80-card').addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const rotateX = ((y - rect.height / 2) / rect.height) * 10;
            const rotateY = ((x - rect.width / 2) / rect.width) * -10;
            
            e.currentTarget.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        document.querySelector('.cr80-card').addEventListener('mouseleave', (e) => {
            e.currentTarget.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
            e.currentTarget.style.transition = "transform 0.5s ease";
        });

        document.querySelector('.cr80-card').addEventListener('mouseenter', (e) => {
            e.currentTarget.style.transition = "none";
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the card following the mouse slightly
        const card = document.querySelector('.monolith-card');
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const xAxis = (window.innerWidth / 2 - e.pageX) / 50;
            const yAxis = (window.innerHeight / 2 - e.pageY) / 50;
            card.style.transform = `rotateY(${xAxis}deg) rotateX(${yAxis}deg)`;
        });

        // Reset transform on mouse leave
        document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = `rotateY(0deg) rotateX(0deg)`;
        });
    </script>

        // Micro-interaction for the glitch border
        const photoContainer = document.querySelector('.glitch-border');
        photoContainer.addEventListener('mouseover', () => {
            photoContainer.style.filter = 'hue-rotate(90deg) brightness(1.2)';
            setTimeout(() => {
                photoContainer.style.filter = 'none';
            }, 150);
        });

        // Interactive scanning line speed change
        const scanline = document.querySelector('.scanline');
        setInterval(() => {
            const randomSpeed = Math.random() * 3 + 1;
            scanline.style.animationDuration = `${randomSpeed}s`;
        }, 5000);
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        // Simple interactive shimmer effect on mouse move
        const card = document.querySelector('.classic-royal-gradient');
        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const shimmer = card.querySelector('.hologram-shimmer');
            shimmer.style.background = `radial-gradient(circle at ${x}px ${y}px, rgba(255,255,255,0.15) 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 70%)`;
        });
        
        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            const shimmer = card.querySelector('.hologram-shimmer');
            shimmer.style.background = `linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0) 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.2) 50%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 100%)`;
        });
    </script>

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const card = document.querySelector('.blueprint-grid');
            
            // Subtle hover effect to simulate a digital screen shine
            card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
                const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
                const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
                
                card.style.background = `
                    radial-gradient(circle at ${x}px ${y}px, rgba(255,255,255,0.08) 0%, transparent 60%),
                    linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) 1px, transparent 1px),
                    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) 1px, transparent 1px),
                    #00327d`;
                card.style.backgroundSize = `auto, 20px 20px, 20px 20px, auto`;
            });

            card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                card.style.background = '';
            });
        });

<script>
        // Subtle micro-interaction: holographic effect follows mouse
        const card = document.querySelector('.matte-black-texture');
        const hologram = document.querySelector('.security-shimmer');

        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const moveX = (x / rect.width) * 20 - 10;
            const moveY = (y / rect.height) * 20 - 10;
            
            hologram.style.transform = `translate(${moveX}px, ${moveY}px) rotate(-15deg)`;
            hologram.style.opacity = (Math.abs(moveX) + Math.abs(moveY)) / 40 + 0.1;
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            hologram.style.transform = `translate(0, 0) rotate(-15deg)`;
            hologram.style.opacity = 0.3;
        });
    </script>
</body></html>

<script>
        // Simple Micro-interaction for the card
        const card = document.querySelector('.group');
        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 20;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 20;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });
        
        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = 'perspective(1000px) rotateX(0) rotateY(0)';
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the card
        const card = document.querySelector('.card-container');
        card.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const { left, top, width, height } = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = (e.clientX - left) / width - 0.5;
            const y = (e.clientY - top) / height - 0.5;
            
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateY(${x * 10}deg) rotateX(${-y * 10}deg)`;
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateY(0deg) rotateX(0deg)`;
            card.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease';
        });

        card.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            card.style.transition = 'none';
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the card following the mouse
        const card = document.querySelector('.cr80-card');
        const container = document.querySelector('.card-perspective');

        container.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 20;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 20;
            
            card.style.transform = `rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        container.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = 'rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)';
        });
    </script>

<script>
        // Micro-interaction for the card
        const card = document.querySelector('.cr80-card');
        const container = document.querySelector('.card-container');

        container.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            const centerX = rect.width / 2;
            const centerY = rect.height / 2;
            
            const rotateX = (y - centerY) / 10;
            const rotateY = (centerX - x) / 10;
            
            card.style.transform = `rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
        });

        container.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            card.style.transform = 'rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)';
            card.style.transition = 'transform 0.5s ease';
        });

        container.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            card.style.transition = 'none';
        });
    </script>

<!-- Atmospheric Micro-interactions -->
<script>
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            const card = document.querySelector('.w-\\[340px\\]');
            const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
            const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
            
            if (x > 0 && x < rect.width && y > 0 && y < rect.height) {
                const rotateX = (y - rect.height / 2) / 25;
                const rotateY = (rect.width / 2 - x) / 25;
                card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg)`;
            } else {
                card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(0deg) rotateY(0deg)`;
            }
        });

        // Add a subtle scanline effect to the photo
        const photoContainer = document.querySelector('.w-\\[140px\\]');
        const scanline = document.createElement('div');
        scanline.style.position = 'absolute';
        scanline.style.top = '0';
        scanline.style.left = '0';
        scanline.style.width = '100%';
        scanline.style.height = '2px';
        scanline.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
        scanline.style.boxShadow = '0 0 4px rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
        scanline.style.zIndex = '20';
        photoContainer.appendChild(scanline);

        let pos = 0;
        function animateScanline() {
            pos += 1.5;
            if (pos > 175) pos = -10;
            scanline.style.top = pos + 'px';
            requestAnimationFrame(animateScanline);
        }
        animateScanline();
    </script>
